# Third-party notices

Prvw is built on open-source software and open data. This file lists every third-party crate and
data set that ships inside the app, with its license, and reproduces the license texts in full.

Generated by `./scripts/check.sh --check third-party-notices` from `Cargo.lock`, `deny.toml`, and
the hand-kept `scripts/check/checks/third-party-vendored.json`. Don't edit it by hand; edit
that list or the generator at `scripts/check/checks/desktop-third-party-notices.go` instead.

- Rust crates: 471
- Data and other files: 2
- Distinct license texts: 232

Prvw links `lensfun` and `rawler` statically. Their licenses (the GNU LGPL) let you replace them with a
modified version: Prvw's own source is public at <https://github.com/vdavid/prvw>, so you can
rebuild the app against your copy. Their own sources are linked in the list below.

## Data and other files

- **LensFun lens database** by The LensFun contributors, CC-BY-SA-3.0, <https://github.com/lensfun/lensfun/tree/master/data/db>
  - License: <https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/3.0/>
  - Changes: None to the data. The `lensfun` crate compiles the XML files in, gzip-compressed, and reads them as published.
  - Ships inside: the `lensfun` crate
- **RawTherapee camera profiles** by Maciej Dworak, Lawrence Lee, Alberto Griggio, Thanatomanic, Morgan Hardwood, and other RawTherapee contributors, LicenseRef-RawTherapee-DCP, <https://github.com/Beep6581/RawTherapee/tree/dev/rtdata/dcpprofiles>
  - License: <https://github.com/Beep6581/RawTherapee/tree/dev/rtdata/dcpprofiles>
  - Changes: None to the profiles. The build compresses the `.dcp` files into one zstd bundle that the app reads at run time.
  - Files: `apps/desktop/build-assets/dcps`

### RawTherapee camera profiles license

Text from: `apps/desktop/build-assets/dcps/LICENSE`

````text
The DCP (DNG Color Profile) files in this directory are sourced from the
RawTherapee project (https://github.com/Beep6581/RawTherapee) and contributed
by their respective authors. RawTherapee is distributed under the GNU General
Public License v3. The DCP files themselves are community-contributed works
created from color-target measurements and distributed within the RawTherapee
project for the benefit of open-source raw processing.

Principal DCP contributors include:
- Maciej Dworak (the majority of profiles in this collection)
- Lawrence Lee
- Alberto Griggio
- Thanatomanic
- Morgan Hardwood
- And other RawTherapee contributors

The color-target RAW files used to generate many of these profiles were
released by their photographers under the CC0 license
(https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/).

Redistribution of these DCP files is consistent with RawTherapee's own
practice of including them in their open-source distribution and packaging.
If you are a DCP author listed here and have concerns about redistribution,
please file an issue at https://github.com/vdavid/prvw.

Source: https://github.com/Beep6581/RawTherapee/tree/dev/rtdata/dcpprofiles
````

## License texts

Each distinct text is reproduced once, with the crates it covers and the file it was read from.
Many differ only in their copyright line, which is exactly what these licenses require to be
carried along.

### Apache-2.0

Covers: ab_glyph, ab_glyph_rasterizer, aws-lc-sys, gl_generator, khronos_api, owned_ttf_parser, spirv, sync_wrapper, ultrahdr-core

struc FGData
    .seed:                      resd 1
    .num_y_points:              resd 1
    .y_points:                  resb 14 * 2
    .chroma_scaling_from_luma:  resd 1
    .num_uv_points:             resd 2
    .uv_points:                 resb 2 * 10 * 2
    .scaling_shift:             resd 1
    .ar_coeff_lag:              resd 1
    .ar_coeffs_y:               resb 24
    .ar_coeffs_uv:              resb 2 * 28 ; includes padding
    .ar_coeff_shift:            resq 1
    .grain_scale_shift:         resd 1
    .uv_mult:                   resd 2
    .uv_luma_mult:              resd 2
    .uv_offset:                 resd 2
    .overlap_flag:              resd 1
    .clip_to_restricted_range:  resd 1
endstruc

cextern gaussian_sequence
````

  library `Frob' (a library for tweaking knobs) written by James Random
  Hacker.

  {signature of Ty Coon}, 1 April 1990
  Ty Coon, President of Vice

That's all there is to it!
````

### MIT

Covers: qoi

Text from: `ext/qoi/qoifuzz.c`

````text
/*

clang fuzzing harness for qoi_decode

Compile and run with: 
	clang -fsanitize=address,fuzzer -g -O0 qoifuzz.c && ./a.out

Dominic Szablewski - https://phoboslab.org


-- LICENSE: The MIT License(MIT)

Copyright(c) 2021 Dominic Szablewski

Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy of
this software and associated documentation files(the "Software"), to deal in
the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights to
use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and / or sell copies
of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is furnished to do
so, subject to the following conditions :
The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all
copies or substantial portions of the Software.
THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR
IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,
FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT.IN NO EVENT SHALL THE
AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER
LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE
SOFTWARE.

*/


#define QOI_IMPLEMENTATION
#include "qoi.h"
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>

int LLVMFuzzerTestOneInput(const uint8_t *data, size_t size) {
	int w, h;
	if (size < 4) {
		return 0;
	}

	qoi_desc desc;
	void* decoded = qoi_decode((void*)(data + 4), (int)(size - 4), &desc, *((int *)data));
	if (decoded != NULL) {
		free(decoded);
	}
	return 0;
}
````
